Extensions and rationale (deadline-based availability evaluation):
- Request generator/availability pipeline: added scripts to run entanglement-service availability sweeps, batch runners (eta threshold, upgrade-k, topology), and normalization/aggregation helpers to compute request-level success metrics.
- Strategy params with eta/eta^2: introduced QT/DQT/EQT strategy parameterization (eta scaling, eta^2 scaling) to model transduction effects and enable eta-threshold comparisons.
- Logging/normalization: added utilities to normalize raw event logs into request CSVs and compute availability/CI summaries for reproducible aggregation.
- Batch runners + plotting: added plotting scripts and summary figure builders to generate publication-ready availability curves, bars, and diagnostics for eta-insensitivity/bottlenecks.
- Topology/upgrade studies: added scripts and analysis to sweep across topologies and upgrade-k, enabling detection of saturated/insensitive regimes (negative results retained).

Why each extension was needed:
- Enable deadline-based availability evaluation on SeQUeNCe-derived entanglement service with explicit eta (transducer efficiency) dependence and reproducible batch execution.
- Support diagnostics (served rate, miss-deadline rate, slack, completion time) to explain eta-insensitivity and bottlenecks beyond headline availability.
- Provide automated aggregation/plotting artifacts suitable for thesis figures/tables without manual post-processing.
